Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a stochastic_analysis algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 597. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 892/1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 597 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the stochastic_analysis structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 892. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
